									<p><span style="color: #003366; font-family: omnes;">18 Temmuz 1956&#8217;da Kocaeli&#8217;de doğdu.</span></p><p><span style="color: #003366; font-family: omnes;">1974 yılında Bursa Kız Öğretmen Lisesinden mezun oldu.</span></p><p><span style="color: #003366; font-family: omnes;">Yükseköğrenimini İstanbul Üniversitesi Edebiyat Fakültesinin Tarih Bölümünde tamamladı.</span></p><p><span style="color: #003366; font-family: omnes;">Evli ve bir çocuk annesidir.</span></p>								</div>

<script data-cfasync="false" src="/cdn-cgi/scripts/5c5dd728/cloudflare-static/email-decode.min.js"></script><script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){

var btn = document.getElementById("mesajToggle");
var panel = document.getElementById("mesajPanel");
var buton = document.getElementById("mesajButon");
var kapat = document.getElementById("mesajKapat");

function panelKontrol(){
if(window.innerWidth > 768){
panel.classList.toggle("aktif");
buton.classList.toggle("panel-acik");
}else{
panel.classList.add("aktif");
buton.classList.add("panel-acik");
}
}

function panelKapat(){
panel.classList.remove("aktif");
buton.classList.remove("panel-acik");
}

btn.addEventListener("click", function(e){
e.preventDefault();
e.stopPropagation();
panelKontrol();
});

panel.addEventListener("click", function(e){
e.stopPropagation();
});

buton.addEventListener("click", function(e){
e.stopPropagation();
});

kapat.addEventListener("click", function(e){
e.stopPropagation();
panelKapat();
});

/* BOŞ ALANA TIKLAYINCA KAPAT */
document.addEventListener("click", function(e){
if(panel.classList.contains("aktif")){
if(!panel.contains(e.target) && !buton.contains(e.target)){
panelKapat();
}
}
});

/* ESC TUŞU İLE KAPAT */
document.addEventListener("keydown", function(e){
if(e.key === "Escape"){
panelKapat();
}
});

});
</script>

				const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
				} );

<script id="wpforms-recaptcha-js-after">
var wpformsDispatchEvent = function (el, ev, custom) {
				var e = document.createEvent(custom ? "CustomEvent" : "HTMLEvents");
				custom ? e.initCustomEvent(ev, true, true, false) : e.initEvent(ev, true, true);
				el.dispatchEvent(e);
			};
		var wpformsRecaptchaCallback = function (el) {
				var hdn = el.parentNode.querySelector(".wpforms-recaptcha-hidden");
				var err = el.parentNode.querySelector("#g-recaptcha-hidden-error");
				hdn.value = "1";
				wpformsDispatchEvent(hdn, "change", false);
				hdn.classList.remove("wpforms-error");
				err && hdn.parentNode.removeChild(err);
			};
		var wpformsRecaptchaLoad = function () {
					Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(".g-recaptcha"), function (el) {
						try {
							var recaptchaID = grecaptcha.render(el, {
								callback: function () {
									wpformsRecaptchaCallback(el);
								}
							});
							el.setAttribute("data-recaptcha-id", recaptchaID);
						} catch (error) {}
					});
					wpformsDispatchEvent(document, "wpformsRecaptchaLoaded", true);
				};
			
//# sourceURL=wpforms-recaptcha-js-after
</script>
